[err-00003] helpful=0 harmful=0 :: Do not equate the verb "sold" with a secondary equity sale. [err-00005] helpful=0 harmful=0 :: Do not map percentages described as a "margin", "basis spread", or "over LIBOR" to interest‑rate‑at‑period‑end tags. Such figures represent a spread over a reference rate and should be tagged with DebtInstrumentBasisSpreadOnVariableRate1.
[err-00007] helpful=0 harmful=0 :: Do not interpret the word "available" or phrases like "availability under the credit facility" as indicating the remaining borrowing capacity. [ctx-00006] helpful=0 harmful=0 :: Keywords indicating a basis spread include "margin", "basis spread", "over LIBOR", "over the reference rate", or phrasing like "X% margin on the variable‑rate loan". [ctx-00008] helpful=0 harmful=0 :: When a figure (e.g., $75 million) is described as the size, limit, or total amount of a revolving credit facility, even if the sentence also mentions "availability" or "available to borrow," treat it as the facility's maximum borrowing capacity and use LineOfCreditFacilityMaximumBorrowingCapacity. Use LineOfCreditFacilityRemainingBorrowingCapacity only when the amount is explicitly stated as the portion still available for borrowing.
[ctx-00010] helpful=0 harmful=0 :: Keywords such as "interest expense on seller notes", "interest charges on debt", "interest on debt instrument" indicate that the appropriate tag is InterestExpenseDebt rather than the generic InterestExpense.
